  .dark[data-theme-preset='parchment-trust-console'] {
    --gt-surface-canvas: #1d1c19;
    --gt-surface-panel: #25231f;
    --gt-surface-raised: #2e2b26;
    --gt-surface-muted: #363228;
    --gt-surface-inverse: #f5f4ed;
    --gt-ink-primary: #f5f1e8;
    --gt-ink-secondary: #c9c1ae;
    --gt-ink-muted: #9f9686;
    --gt-ink-inverse: #1d1c19;
    --gt-ink-disabled: #6f675a;
    --gt-border-subtle: #38342d;
    --gt-border-default: #605947;
    --gt-border-strong: #d4c6a5;
    --gt-focus-ring: #d7a06d;
    --gt-shadow-panel: #0f0e0c;
    --gt-shadow-overlay: rgb(245 244 237 / 0.12);
    --gt-accent-primary: #d97757;
    --gt-accent-hover: #e68b6e;
    --gt-status-success: #74b18a;
    --gt-status-warning: #d4a647;
    --gt-status-danger: #e0765f;
    --gt-status-info: #8faac0;
    --gt-chart-primary: #f5f1e8;
    --gt-chart-secondary: #c2b7a0;
    --gt-chart-grid: color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-border-strong) 18%, transparent);
    --color-status-success-soft: #8bc79e;
    --color-status-success-bar: #9bd3ac;
    --color-status-warning-soft: #e0b65b;
    --color-status-warning-bar: #efc76e;
    --color-status-danger-soft: #e6806b;
    --color-status-danger-bar: #f0927f;
    --color-chart-primary-area: #d4c6a5;
    --color-chart-secondary-area: #8f856f;
    --color-chart-peak: #d97757;
    --color-chart-attribution: #8faac0;
  }

  html {
    font-size: calc(16px * var(--gt-text-scale-factor));
  }

  body {
  background-color: var(--bg-main);
  color: var(--text-primary);
  transition-property: color, background-color, border-color, text-decoration-color, fill, stroke;
  transition-timing-function: cubic-bezier(0.4, 0, 0.2, 1);
  transition-duration: 200ms;
    font-family: var(--font-family-ui);
    font-size: var(--font-size-ui-lg);
    line-height: var(--line-height-ui-normal);
}
.\!container {
  width: 100% !important;
}
.container {
  width: 100%;
}
@media (min-width: 640px) {

  .\!container {
    max-width: 640px !important;
  }

  .container {
    max-width: 640px;
  }
}
@media (min-width: 768px) {

  .\!container {
    max-width: 768px !important;
  }

  .container {
    max-width: 768px;
  }
}
@media (min-width: 1024px) {

  .\!container {
    max-width: 1024px !important;
  }

  .container {
    max-width: 1024px;
  }
}
@media (min-width: 1280px) {

  .\!container {
    max-width: 1280px !important;
  }

  .container {
    max-width: 1280px;
  }
}
@media (min-width: 1536px) {

  .\!container {
    max-width: 1536px !important;
  }

  .container {
    max-width: 1536px;
  }
}
.btn-swiss {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    gap: 0.5rem;
    padding: 0.5rem 1rem;
    border: 2px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2px;
    background-color: var(--bg-main);
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font-weight: 700;
    font-size: var(--font-size-ui-md);
    letter-spacing: 0.05em;
    box-shadow: 4px 4px 0px 0px var(--shadow-color);
    transition: all 75ms;
    cursor: pointer;
  }
.btn-swiss:hover {
    transform: translate(-1px, -1px);
    box-shadow: 6px 6px 0px 0px var(--shadow-color);
  }
.btn-swiss:active {
    transform: translate(2px, 2px);
    box-shadow: none;
  }
.card-swiss {
    border: 2px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 0px;
    background-color: var(--bg-main);
    padding: 1rem;
    box-shadow: 4px 4px 0px 0px var(--shadow-color);
  }
.settings-trust-console,
  .design-system-trust-console {
    background:
      radial-gradient(circle at 14% 10%, color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-accent-primary) 10%, transparent) 0 12rem, transparent 18rem),
      linear-gradient(135deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-border-strong) 6%, transparent) 0 1px, transparent 1px),
      var(--bg-surface);
    background-size: auto, 18px 18px, auto;
  }
.settings-trust-console .card-swiss,
  .design-system-trust-console .card-swiss {
    border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 48%, transparent);
    background:
      linear-gradient(180deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-surface-raised) 78%, transparent), var(--bg-main)),
      var(--bg-main);
    box-shadow:
      0 1px 0 color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-surface-raised) 70%, transparent) inset,
      0 18px 40px color-mix(in srgb, var(--shadow-color) 42%, transparent);
  }
.settings-trust-hero,
  .design-system-trust-header {
    position: relative;
    overflow: hidden;
    border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 52%, transparent);
    background:
      linear-gradient(90deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-accent-primary) 14%, transparent), transparent 42%),
      linear-gradient(180deg, var(--bg-main), color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-surface) 78%, var(--bg-main)));
    box-shadow: 0 18px 42px color-mix(in srgb, var(--shadow-color) 45%, transparent);
  }
.settings-trust-hero::after,
  .design-system-trust-header::after {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    right: 1.5rem;
    bottom: 1rem;
    width: min(42rem, 52vw);
    height: 1px;
    background: linear-gradient(90deg, transparent, color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-accent-primary) 66%, transparent));
  }
.settings-trust-header,
  .design-system-trust-header {
    border-bottom: 0;
    padding: 1.35rem 1.45rem 1.1rem;
  }
.settings-trust-title,
  .design-system-trust-title {
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font-size: clamp(2rem, 3.2vw, 3.45rem);
    font-weight: 950;
    font-style: italic;
    letter-spacing: -0.07em;
    line-height: 0.9;
    text-transform: uppercase;
  }
.settings-trust-subtitle,
  .design-system-trust-subtitle {
    margin-top: 0.75rem;
    max-width: min(58rem, 72vw);
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-size: var(--font-size-ui-sm);
    font-weight: 850;
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    line-height: 1.45;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    white-space: normal;
  }
.settings-trust-index {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: minmax(10rem, 15rem) minmax(0, 1fr);
    gap: 1rem;
    align-items: end;
    border-top: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 36%, transparent);
    padding: 0.9rem 1.45rem 1.15rem;
  }
.settings-trust-index-kicker,
  .settings-trust-summary span,
  .design-system-section-header p {
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-size: var(--font-size-ui-xs);
    font-weight: 900;
    letter-spacing: 0.12em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
  }
.settings-trust-index-title {
    margin-top: 0.3rem;
    font-size: var(--font-size-ui-xl);
    font-weight: 950;
    font-style: italic;
    letter-spacing: -0.04em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
  }
.settings-trust-index-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(5, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 0.55rem;
  }
.settings-trust-index-button {
    display: flex;
    min-height: 2.7rem;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    gap: 0.6rem;
    border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 45%, transparent);
    background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-main) 88%, var(--gt-surface-muted));
    color: var(--text-primary);
    padding: 0.45rem 0.6rem;
    font-size: var(--font-size-ui-xs);
    font-weight: 950;
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    transition: border-color 140ms ease, transform 140ms ease, background-color 140ms ease;
  }
.settings-trust-index-button span {
    color: var(--gt-accent-primary);
    font-family: var(--font-family-mono);
  }
.settings-trust-index-button:hover {
    transform: translateY(-1px);
    border-color: var(--gt-accent-primary);
    background-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-accent-primary) 9%, var(--bg-main));
  }
.settings-trust-summary {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 0.75rem;
  }
.settings-trust-summary div {
    border: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 36%, transparent);
    background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-main) 70%, var(--bg-surface));
    padding: 0.8rem 0.9rem;
    box-shadow: 0 10px 24px color-mix(in srgb, var(--shadow-color) 32%, transparent);
  }
.settings-trust-summary strong {
    display: block;
    margin-top: 0.35rem;
    overflow: hidden;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font-size: var(--font-size-ui-md);
    font-weight: 950;
    letter-spacing: 0.03em;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
.settings-trust-section > .flex:first-child {
    border-left: 3px solid var(--gt-accent-primary);
    padding-left: 0.65rem;
  }
.settings-trust-section .card-swiss {
    overflow: hidden;
  }
.settings-trust-appearance-grid,
  .settings-trust-runtime-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 0.96fr) minmax(0, 1.24fr);
  }
.settings-trust-field {
    position: relative;
    min-width: 0;
    border-right: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 28%, transparent);
    border-bottom: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 28%, transparent);
    background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-main) 82%, var(--bg-surface));
  }
.settings-trust-field:nth-last-child(-n + 2) {
    border-bottom: 0;
  }
.settings-trust-field:nth-child(even) {
    border-right: 0;
  }
.settings-trust-field-featured,
  .settings-trust-field-wide {
    background:
      linear-gradient(135deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-accent-primary) 8%, transparent), transparent 58%),
      color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-main) 86%, var(--bg-surface));
  }
.settings-trust-field-wide {
    grid-column: 1 / -1;
    border-right: 0;
  }
.settings-trust-console label {
    letter-spacing: 0.08em !important;
  }
.settings-trust-console [data-theme-preset-option],
  .design-system-trust-console [data-theme-preset-option] {
    border-width: 1px;
    box-shadow: none;
  }
.settings-trust-console [data-theme-preset-option][aria-pressed='true'],
  .design-system-trust-console [data-theme-preset-option][aria-pressed='true'] {
    box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-accent-primary) 62%, transparent);
  }
.design-system-trust-console {
    background:
      radial-gradient(circle at 82% 8%, color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-status-info) 9%, transparent) 0 12rem, transparent 19rem),
      linear-gradient(135deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-border-strong) 6%, transparent) 0 1px, transparent 1px),
      var(--bg-surface);
    background-size: auto, 20px 20px, auto;
  }
.design-system-trust-header {
    display: flex;
    align-items: flex-end;
    gap: 1.25rem;
  }
.design-system-action-dock {
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: wrap;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: flex-end;
    gap: 0.55rem;
  }
.design-system-action-dock .btn-swiss,
  .settings-trust-console .btn-swiss {
    border-width: 1px;
    box-shadow: 0 10px 20px color-mix(in srgb, var(--shadow-color) 30%, transparent);
  }
.design-system-hero-grid {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(17rem, 0.34fr);
    gap: 1rem;
  }
.design-system-command-card {
    padding: 1.25rem;
  }
.design-system-command-card .grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
.design-system-info-row {
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: minmax(6rem, 9rem) minmax(0, 1fr);
    align-items: start;
    border-width: 1px;
    background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-main) 74%, var(--bg-surface));
  }
.design-system-info-row code {
    white-space: normal;
    word-break: break-word;
  }
.design-system-coverage-card {
    padding: 1.25rem;
  }
.design-system-section-header {
    border-bottom: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 38%, transparent);
    background:
      linear-gradient(90deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-accent-primary) 8%, transparent), transparent 52%),
      color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-main) 74%, var(--bg-surface));
  }
.design-system-theme-grid {
    background:
      linear-gradient(180deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-surface) 72%, transparent), transparent),
      var(--bg-surface);
  }
.design-system-theme-card {
    min-height: 11rem;
  }
.design-system-theme-card.is-active {
    position: relative;
  }
.design-system-theme-card.is-active::before {
    content: '';
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    border-top: 3px solid var(--gt-accent-primary);
    pointer-events: none;
  }
.design-system-current-preset {
    border-width: 1px;
    background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-accent-primary) 10%, var(--bg-main));
    color: var(--text-primary);
  }
.design-system-business-grid {
    background-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-surface) 88%, var(--bg-main));
  }
.design-system-preview-intro {
    border-width: 1px;
    background:
      linear-gradient(90deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--gt-accent-primary) 7%, transparent), transparent 48%),
      var(--bg-main);
  }
.design-system-story-list {
    border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 32%, transparent);
  }
.design-system-story-list > div {
    background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-main) 82%, var(--bg-surface));
  }
.design-system-story-list > div:nth-child(even) {
    background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-main) 62%, var(--bg-surface));
  }
.design-system-trust-console .divide-y-2 > :not([hidden]) ~ :not([hidden]) {
    border-top-width: 1px;
  }
.design-system-trust-console .border-2,
  .settings-trust-console .border-2 {
    border-width: 1px;
  }
.design-system-trust-console .\!border-2,
  .settings-trust-console .\!border-2 {
    border-width: 1px !important;
  }
@media (max-width: 1100px) {
    .settings-trust-index,
    .design-system-trust-header,
    .design-system-hero-grid {
      grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    .design-system-trust-header {
      align-items: stretch;
    }

    .settings-trust-index-grid,
    .settings-trust-summary {
      grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    .settings-trust-appearance-grid,
    .settings-trust-runtime-grid {
      grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }

    .settings-trust-field {
      border-right: 0;
    }

    .settings-trust-field:nth-last-child(-n + 2) {
      border-bottom: 1px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 28%, transparent);
    }
  }
@media (max-width: 720px) {
    .settings-trust-console,
    .design-system-trust-console {
      padding: 1rem;
    }

    .settings-trust-index-grid,
    .settings-trust-summary {
      grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    }
  }
.codex-live-session-list-item-selected {
    background-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--text-primary) 12%, var(--bg-main));
    box-shadow: inset 3px 0 0 var(--text-primary);
  }
.codex-live-session-list-item-idle:hover {
    background-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 5%, var(--bg-main));
  }
.usage-desk-control-bar {
    container-type: inline-size;
    display: flex;
    flex-wrap: nowrap;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: space-between;
    width: 100%;
    gap: 0.75rem;
    border-bottom: 1px solid var(--shadow-color);
    background-color: var(--bg-main);
    padding: 0.625rem 1rem;
  }
.usage-desk-control-cluster {
    display: flex;
    min-width: 0;
    flex: 0 1 auto;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: flex-end;
    gap: 0.75rem;
    margin-left: auto;
  }
.usage-desk-range-slot {
    position: relative;
    display: flex;
    height: 2rem;
    width: 15.5rem;
    flex: 0 0 15.5rem;
    align-items: center;
    min-width: 0;
  }
.usage-desk-range-layer {
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    transition: opacity 180ms ease, transform 180ms ease;
  }
.usage-desk-range-layer.is-visible {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateX(0);
    pointer-events: auto;
  }
.usage-desk-range-layer.is-hidden-left {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateX(-0.75rem);
    pointer-events: none;
  }
.usage-desk-range-layer.is-hidden-right {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateX(0.75rem);
    pointer-events: none;
  }
.usage-desk-segmented {
    display: flex;
    height: 2rem;
    min-width: 0;
    flex: 0 0 auto;
    align-items: stretch;
    border: 2px solid var(--border-color);
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    padding: 0.125rem;
  }
.usage-desk-range-slot .usage-desk-segmented {
    width: 100%;
  }
.usage-desk-segment {
    display: flex;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    min-width: 0;
    white-space: nowrap;
    border: 0;
    background-color: transparent;
    padding: 0 0.625rem;
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font-size: var(--font-size-ui-md-compact);
    font-weight: 900;
    line-height: 1;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    transition: background-color 120ms ease, color 120ms ease, opacity 120ms ease;
  }
.usage-desk-range-slot .usage-desk-segment {
    flex: 1 1 0;
    padding-right: 0;
    padding-left: 0;
  }
.usage-desk-segment.is-active {
    background-color: var(--text-primary);
    color: var(--bg-main);
    opacity: 1;
  }
.usage-desk-segment.is-muted {
    opacity: 0.42;
  }
.usage-desk-segment.is-muted:hover {
    opacity: 1;
  }
.usage-desk-overflow-menu {
    position: relative;
    display: none;
    flex: 0 0 auto;
  }
.usage-desk-overflow-menu > summary {
    list-style: none;
  }
.usage-desk-overflow-menu > summary::-webkit-details-marker {
    display: none;
  }
.usage-desk-overflow-trigger {
    display: flex;
    width: 2rem;
    height: 2rem;
    cursor: pointer;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    border: 2px solid var(--border-color);
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
    color: var(--text-primary);
  }
.usage-desk-overflow-menu[open] .usage-desk-overflow-trigger {
    background-color: var(--text-primary);
    color: var(--bg-main);
  }
.usage-desk-overflow-panel {
    position: absolute;
    top: calc(100% + 0.5rem);
    right: 0;
    z-index: 50;
    display: grid;
    min-width: 11rem;
    gap: 0.5rem;
    border: 2px solid var(--border-color);
    background-color: var(--bg-main);
    padding: 0.5rem;
    box-shadow: 4px 4px 0 var(--shadow-color);
  }
.usage-desk-overflow-section {
    display: none;
    gap: 0.25rem;
  }
.usage-desk-overflow-heading {
    padding: 0.125rem 0.375rem;
    color: var(--text-muted);
    font-size: var(--font-size-ui-xs);
    font-weight: 900;
    text-transform: uppercase;
  }
.usage-desk-overflow-item {
    display: flex;
    width: 100%;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: flex-start;
    border: 0;
    background-color: transparent;
    padding: 0.5rem 0.625rem;
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font-size: var(--font-size-ui-md-compact);
    font-weight: 900;
    text-align: left;
    text-transform: uppercase;
  }
.usage-desk-overflow-item.is-active {
    background-color: var(--text-primary);
    color: var(--bg-main);
  }
@container (max-width: 760px) {
    .usage-desk-secondary-surface-option {
      display: none;
    }

    .usage-desk-projected-overflow-menu {
      display: block;
    }

    .usage-desk-surface-overflow-section {
      display: grid;
    }
  }
@container (max-width: 620px) {
    .usage-desk-metric-group {
      display: none;
    }

    .usage-desk-metric-overflow-section {
      display: grid;
    }
  }
@container (max-width: 520px) {
    .usage-desk-range-slot {
      display: none;
    }

    .usage-desk-compact-overflow-menu {
      display: block;
    }

    .usage-desk-range-overflow-section {
      display: grid;
    }
  }
[data-account-card] {
    container-type: inline-size;
  }
[data-account-card] * {
    min-width: 0;
  }
.account-card-grid-full {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(auto-fill, minmax(min(100%, var(--account-card-grid-full-min-width)), 1fr));
    justify-content: start;
  }
.account-card-list-row {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
  }
.account-card-list-metrics,
  .account-card-flow-head {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
.account-card-billing-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
.account-card-quota-row {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
  }
.account-card-rate-limit-row {
    grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
  }
.account-card-quota-refresh-skeleton {
    background-image:
      repeating-linear-gradient(
        115deg,
        color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-main) 0%, transparent) 0 7px,
        color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-main) 60%, transparent) 7px 12px,
        color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 18%, transparent) 12px 15px,
        color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg-main) 0%, transparent) 15px 24px
      );
    background-size: 32px 100%;
    animation: account-card-quota-refresh-sweep 0.85s linear infinite;
    opacity: 0.72;
  }
.account-card-action-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
.account-card-action-grid-span {
    grid-column: 1 / -1;
  }
@container (min-width: 46rem) {
    .account-card-list-row {
      align-items: center;
      grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
    }

    .account-card-list-actions {
      justify-self: end;
    }
  }
@container (max-width: 320px) {
    .account-card-traffic {
      grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
    }

    .account-card-traffic-chart {
      border-left: 0;
      border-top: 2px solid var(--border-color);
      padding-left: 0;
      padding-top: 0.75rem;
    }

    .account-card-list-metric-cell {
      border-left: 0;
      border-bottom: 1px dashed var(--border-color);
    }

    .account-card-list-metric-cell:last-child {
      border-bottom: 0;
    }

    .account-card-usage-metrics {
      grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
    }

    .account-card-billing-grid {
      grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr);
    }

  }
.input-swiss {
    padding: 0.5rem 0.75rem;
    border: 2px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2px;
    background-color: var(--bg-main);
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font-size: var(--font-size-ui-lg);
  }
.select-swiss {
    -webkit-appearance: none;
       -moz-appearance: none;
            appearance: none;
    width: 100%;
    padding: 0.5rem 2.75rem 0.5rem 0.75rem;
    border: 2px solid var(--border-color);
    border-radius: 2px;
    background-color: var(--bg-main);
    color: var(--text-primary);
    font-size: var(--font-size-ui-lg);
    font-weight: 700;
    letter-spacing: 0.08em;
    cursor: pointer;
    transition: background-color 75ms, transform 75ms, box-shadow 75ms;
    box-shadow: 4px 4px 0px 0px var(--shadow-color);
  }
.select-swiss:hover {
    transform: translate(-1px, -1px);
    box-shadow: 6px 6px 0px 0px var(--shadow-color);
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
  }
.select-swiss:focus {
    outline: none;
    background-color: var(--bg-surface);
  }
.codex-success-hud {
    animation: codex-success-hud-in 160ms cubic-bezier(0.2, 0.9, 0.2, 1), codex-success-hud-out 180ms ease-in 1620ms forwards;
  }
.codex-success-hud-icon {
    animation: codex-success-hud-check 520ms cubic-bezier(0.22, 1.3, 0.36, 1);
  }
.codex-success-hud-ring {
    animation: codex-success-hud-ring 720ms ease-out;
  }
.scrollbar-stable {
    scrollbar-gutter: stable;
  }
[data-design-system-inspect-mode='active'] [data-design-system-highlight='project'] [data-design-system-component='true'] {
    position: relative;
    outline: 2px solid var(--accent-red);
    outline-offset: 2px;
  }
[data-design-system-inspect-mode='active'] [data-design-system-highlight='project'] [data-design-system-component='true'][data-design-system-component-name]::before {
    content: attr(data-design-system-component-name);
    position: absolute;
    left: -2px;
    top: -1.45rem;
    z-index: 40;
    max-width: min(13rem, calc(100vw - 2rem));
    overflow: hidden;
    border: 2px solid var(--accent-red);
    background: var(--accent-red);
    color: var(--bg-main);
    cursor: copy;
    font-family: var(--font-family-mono);
    font-size: var(--font-size-ui-xs);
    font-weight: 900;
    letter-spacing: 0;
    line-height: 1;
    padding: 0.25rem 0.35rem;
    pointer-events: auto;
    text-overflow: ellipsis;
    text-transform: uppercase;
    white-space: nowrap;
  }
[data-design-system-inspect-mode='active'] [data-design-system-highlight='project'] [data-design-system-component='true'][data-design-system-component-copied='true']::before {
    content: attr(data-design-system-component-name) ' / COPIED';
    background: var(--color-status-success);
    border-color: var(--color-status-success);
  }
[data-design-system-inspect-mode='active'] [data-design-system-highlight='project'] [data-design-system-component='true'][data-design-system-component-copied='error']::before {
    content: attr(data-design-system-component-name) ' / COPY FAILED';
    background: var(--accent-red);
    border-color: var(--accent-red);
  }
[data-design-system-inspect-mode='active'] [data-design-system-highlight='project'] [data-codex-feature-config-panel='true'] [data-design-system-component='true'] {
    outline: none;
  }
[data-design-system-inspect-mode='active'] [data-design-system-highlight='project'] [data-codex-feature-config-panel='true'] [data-design-system-component='true'][data-design-system-component-name]::before {
    content: none;
    display: none;
  }
.page-loading-panel {
    min-height: 7rem;
    position: relative;
  }
.page-loading-panel::before,
  .page-loading-panel::after {
    position: absolute;
    content: '';
    inset: 0;
    z-index: 20;
    pointer-events: none;
    border: 2px solid var(--text-primary);
    background:
      linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0 18%, var(--text-primary) 18% 20%, transparent 20% 100%),
      linear-gradient(180deg, transparent 0 64%, var(--text-primary) 64% 66%, transparent 66% 100%);
    opacity: 0;
    animation: page-loading-clone 1.18s steps(1, end) infinite;
  }
.page-loading-panel::before {
    --clone-x: 0.42rem;
    --clone-y: -0.2rem;
    --clone-opacity: 0.18;
  }
.page-loading-panel::after {
    --clone-x: -0.34rem;
    --clone-y: 0.26rem;
    --clone-opacity: 0.12;
    animation-delay: -0.38s;
  }
.page-loading-rail-cell {
    border-bottom: 2px solid var(--bg-main);
    animation: page-loading-rail 1.2s steps(2, end) infinite;
  }
.page-loading-rail-bus {
    position: absolute;
    inset: 0;
    z-index: 1;
    opacity: 0.34;
    background-image:
      repeating-linear-gradient(
        180deg,
        transparent 0 5px,
        var(--bg-main) 5px 7px,
        transparent 7px 12px
      ),
      linear-gradient(90deg, transparent 0 42%, var(--bg-main) 42% 48%, transparent 48% 100%);
    background-size: 100% 24px, 100% 100%;
    animation: page-loading-bus 0.45s linear infinite;
  }
.page-loading-rail-cell:nth-of-type(2) {
    animation-delay: 0.15s;
  }
.page-loading-rail-cell:nth-of-type(3) {
    animation-delay: 0.3s;
  }
.page-loading-rail-cell:nth-of-type(4) {
    animation-delay: 0.45s;
    border-bottom: 0;
  }
.page-loading-orbit {
    position: absolute;
    left: 50%;
    top: 50%;
    z-index: 2;
    width: 3.25rem;
    height: 3.25rem;
    transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
  }
.page-loading-planet {
    position: absolute;
    left: 50%;
    top: 50%;
    z-index: 2;
    width: 0.62rem;
    height: 0.62rem;
    border: 2px solid var(--bg-main);
    background: var(--bg-main);
    transform: translate(-50%, -50%);
  }
.page-loading-orbit-line {
    position: absolute;
    border: 1.5px solid var(--bg-main);
    border-left-color: transparent;
    border-radius: 9999px;
    opacity: 0.92;
    animation: page-loading-orbit-spin 1.15s linear infinite;
  }
.page-loading-orbit-line::after {
    position: absolute;
    content: '';
    top: 0.12rem;
    right: 0.18rem;
    width: 0.22rem;
    height: 0.22rem;
    background: var(--bg-main);
  }
.page-loading-orbit-line-a {
    inset: 0.1rem;
    --page-loading-orbit-start: 0deg;
  }
.page-loading-orbit-line-b {
    inset: 0.42rem;
    --page-loading-orbit-start: 64deg;
    opacity: 0.68;
    animation-duration: 1.45s;
  }
.page-loading-orbit-line-c {
    inset: 0.74rem;
    --page-loading-orbit-start: -38deg;
    opacity: 0.5;
    animation-duration: 1.85s;
  }
.page-loading-influx {
    z-index: 0;
    background-image:
      linear-gradient(90deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 10%, transparent) 1px, transparent 1px),
      linear-gradient(180deg, color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 10%, transparent) 1px, transparent 1px);
    background-size: 18px 100%, 100% 14px;
  }
.page-loading-influx > span {
    position: absolute;
    right: -2rem;
    top: var(--packet-top);
    width: var(--packet-width);
    height: 0.25rem;
    background: var(--text-primary);
    opacity: 0;
    animation: page-loading-influx var(--packet-duration) cubic-bezier(0.76, 0, 0.24, 1) infinite;
    animation-delay: var(--packet-delay);
  }
.page-loading-influx > span::after {
    position: absolute;
    content: '';
    right: -0.55rem;
    top: -0.13rem;
    width: 0.38rem;
    height: 0.5rem;
    border: 1px solid var(--text-primary);
    background: var(--bg-main);
  }
.page-loading-bitstream {
    z-index: 1;
    overflow: hidden;
    opacity: 0.26;
    font-family: ui-monospace, SFMono-Regular, Menlo, Monaco, Consolas, 'Liberation Mono', monospace;
    font-size: 0.42rem;
    font-weight: 900;
    line-height: 1;
    letter-spacing: 0.18em;
    color: var(--text-primary);
  }
.page-loading-bitstream > span {
    position: absolute;
    left: 0;
    width: -moz-max-content;
    width: max-content;
    animation: page-loading-bitstream 1.15s linear infinite;
  }
.page-loading-bitstream > span::before {
    content: '011010 110001 001101 101110 010111 111000 001011';
  }
.page-loading-bitstream > span:nth-child(1) {
    top: 1.15rem;
    animation-delay: -0.22s;
  }
.page-loading-bitstream > span:nth-child(2) {
    top: 3.36rem;
    animation-duration: 0.92s;
    animation-delay: -0.7s;
  }
.page-loading-bitstream > span:nth-child(3) {
    top: 5.54rem;
    animation-duration: 1.32s;
    animation-delay: -0.44s;
  }
.page-loading-scan {
    animation: page-loading-scan 1.05s cubic-bezier(0.76, 0, 0.24, 1) infinite;
  }
.page-loading-grid {
    background-image: repeating-linear-gradient(
      90deg,
      transparent 0 12px,
      color-mix(in srgb, var(--border-color) 24%, transparent) 12px 14px
    );
  }
.page-loading-step {
    transform-origin: bottom;
    animation: page-loading-step 1s steps(2, end) infinite;
  }
.page-loading-step:nth-child(2) {
    animation-delay: 0.12s;
  }
.page-loading-step:nth-child(3) {
    animation-delay: 0.24s;
  }
.page-loading-step:nth-child(4) {
    animation-delay: 0.36s;
  }
.page-loading-step:nth-child(5) {
    animation-delay: 0.48s;
  }
.page-loading-pulse {
    animation: page-loading-pulse 0.9s steps(2, end) infinite;
  }
.page-loading-ticker::after {
    content: '03';
    animation: page-loading-ticker 1.8s steps(1, end) infinite;
  }
.page-loading-ticker {
    font-size: var(--font-size-ui-xs);
  }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
    .page-loading-rail-cell,
    .page-loading-rail-bus,
    .page-loading-panel::before,
    .page-loading-panel::after,
    .page-loading-orbit-line,
    .page-loading-influx > span,
    .page-loading-bitstream > span,
    .page-loading-scan,
    .page-loading-step,
    .page-loading-pulse {
      animation: none;
    }

    .page-loading-scan {
      transform: translateX(100%);
    }

    .page-loading-ticker::after {
      animation: none;
      content: '03';
    }

    .page-loading-influx > span {
      opacity: 0.16;
      transform: translateX(0) scaleX(1);
    }
  }

@keyframes account-card-quota-refresh-sweep {
  from {
    background-position: 0 0;
  }
  to {
    background-position: 32px 0;
  }
}

@keyframes page-loading-scan {
  0% {
    transform: translateX(-105%);
  }
  100% {
    transform: translateX(305%);
  }
}

@keyframes page-loading-step {
  0%,
  100% {
    opacity: 0.28;
    transform: scaleY(0.55);
  }
  45% {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: scaleY(1);
  }
}

@keyframes page-loading-rail {
  0%,
  100% {
    opacity: 0.18;
  }
  50% {
    opacity: 1;
  }
}

@keyframes page-loading-orbit-spin {
  from {
    transform: rotate(var(--page-loading-orbit-start, 0deg));
  }
  to {
    transform: rotate(calc(var(--page-loading-orbit-start, 0deg) + 360deg));
  }
}

@keyframes page-loading-pulse {
  0%,
  100% {
    transform: scale(0.72);
  }
  50% {
    transform: scale(1);
  }
}

@keyframes page-loading-ticker {
  0%,
  19% {
    content: '01';
  }
  20%,
  39% {
    content: '02';
  }
  40%,
  59% {
    content: '03';
  }
  60%,
  79% {
    content: '04';
  }
  80%,
  100% {
    content: '05';
  }
}

@keyframes page-loading-influx {
  0% {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateX(125%) scaleX(0.45);
  }
  12% {
    opacity: var(--packet-opacity, 0.32);
  }
  64% {
    opacity: calc(var(--packet-opacity, 0.32) * 0.62);
  }
  100% {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateX(-105%) scaleX(1.1);
  }
}

@keyframes page-loading-bus {
  from {
    background-position: 0 0;
  }
  to {
    background-position: 0 24px;
  }
}

@keyframes page-loading-bitstream {
  from {
    transform: translateX(105%);
  }
  to {
    transform: translateX(-115%);
  }
}

@keyframes page-loading-clone {
  0%,
  62%,
  100% {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translate(0, 0);
  }
  66% {
    opacity: var(--clone-opacity, 0.22);
    transform: translate(var(--clone-x, 0.45rem), var(--clone-y, -0.25rem));
  }
  70% {
    opacity: 0.06;
    transform: translate(calc(var(--clone-x, 0.45rem) * -0.45), calc(var(--clone-y, -0.25rem) * -0.6));
  }
}

@keyframes codex-success-hud-in {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(-0.75rem) scale(0.96);
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateY(0) scale(1);
  }
}

@keyframes codex-success-hud-out {
  to {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(-0.5rem) scale(0.98);
  }
}

@keyframes codex-success-hud-check {
  0% {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: scale(0.5) rotate(-12deg);
  }
  55% {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: scale(1.16) rotate(0deg);
  }
  100% {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: scale(1) rotate(0deg);
  }
}

@keyframes codex-success-hud-ring {
  0% {
    opacity: 0.45;
    transform: scale(0.92);
  }
  100% {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: scale(1.45);
  }
}

  .xl\:pl-6 {
    padding-left: 1.5rem;
  }

  .xl\:pr-5 {
    padding-right: 1.25rem;
  }
}
@media (min-width: 1536px) {

  .\32xl\:col-span-2 {
    grid-column: span 2 / span 2;
  }

  .\32xl\:grid-cols-2 {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
}
.\[\&\:\:-webkit-details-marker\]\:hidden::-webkit-details-marker {
  display: none;
}
@media (min-width: 1024px) {

  .lg\:\[\&\:nth-child\(4n\)\]\:border-r-0:nth-child(4n) {
    border-right-width: 0px;
  }
}
.\[\&_blockquote\]\:border-l-2 blockquote {
  border-left-width: 2px;
}
.\[\&_blockquote\]\:border-\[var\(--border-color\)\] blockquote {
  border-color: var(--border-color);
}
.\[\&_blockquote\]\:pl-3 blockquote {
  padding-left: 0.75rem;
}
.\[\&_code\]\:font-mono code {
  font-family: var(--font-family-mono);
}
.\[\&_code\]\:font-black code {
  font-weight: 900;
}
.\[\&_h1\]\:mb-4 h1 {
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
}
.\[\&_h1\]\:font-mono h1 {
  font-family: var(--font-family-mono);
}
.\[\&_h1\]\:text-xl h1 {
  font-size: var(--font-size-ui-4xl);
  line-height: var(--line-height-ui-tight);
}
.\[\&_h1\]\:font-black h1 {
  font-weight: 900;
}
.\[\&_h1\]\:italic h1 {
  font-style: italic;
}
.\[\&_h2\]\:mb-3 h2 {
  margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
}
.\[\&_h2\]\:mt-5 h2 {
  margin-top: 1.25rem;
}
.\[\&_h2\]\:font-mono h2 {
  font-family: var(--font-family-mono);
}
.\[\&_h2\]\:text-base h2 {
  font-size: var(--font-size-ui-2xl);
  line-height: var(--line-height-ui-normal);
}
.\[\&_h2\]\:font-black h2 {
  font-weight: 900;
}
.\[\&_h3\]\:mb-2 h3 {
  margin-bottom: 0.5rem;
}
.\[\&_h3\]\:mt-4 h3 {
  margin-top: 1rem;
}
.\[\&_h3\]\:font-mono h3 {
  font-family: var(--font-family-mono);
}
.\[\&_h3\]\:font-black h3 {
  font-weight: 900;
}
.\[\&_li\]\:ml-5 li {
  margin-left: 1.25rem;
}
.\[\&_ol\]\:list-decimal ol {
  list-style-type: decimal;
}
.\[\&_p\]\:mb-3 p {
  margin-bottom: 0.75rem;
}
.\[\&_pre\]\:mb-4 pre {
  margin-bottom: 1rem;
}
.\[\&_pre\]\:overflow-auto pre {
  overflow: auto;
}
.\[\&_pre\]\:border-2 pre {
  border-width: 2px;
}
.\[\&_pre\]\:border-\[var\(--border-color\)\] pre {
  border-color: var(--border-color);
}
.\[\&_pre\]\:bg-\[var\(--bg-main\)\] pre {
  background-color: var(--bg-main);
}
.\[\&_pre\]\:p-3 pre {
  padding: 0.75rem;
}
.\[\&_ul\]\:list-disc ul {
  list-style-type: disc;
}
@media(hover:hover) {

  .\[\@media\(hover\:hover\)\]\:hover\:border-\[var\(--text-primary\)\]:hover {
    border-color: var(--text-primary);
  }
}
</style></head>
